# Neymar

For other uses, see [Neymar (disambiguation)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neymar_\(disambiguation\)).

In this [Portuguese name](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portuguese_name), the first or maternal [family name](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Surname) is *da Silva* and the second or paternal family name is *Santos*. *"Júnior"* is a [generational suffix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Generational_suffix)  used for someone whose name is the same as their father, like "Jr." in English.

**Neymar da Silva Santos Júnior**[^cite_note-3] (born 5 February 1992), known [mononymously](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mononym) as **Neymar**, is a Brazilian professional [footballer](10568.md) who plays as an [attacking midfielder](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attacking_midfielder) or a [forward](428681.md) for and [captains](633462.md) [Campeonato Brasileiro Série A](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Campeonato_Brasileiro_S%C3%A9rie_A) club [Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santos_FC). A goalscorer and playmaker, he is known for his dribbling, technical ability, agility, passing, and finishing. Often regarded as one of the greatest players of all time, Neymar is both the [all-time top goalscorer](149286.md#Top_goalscorers) ([80](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_international_goals_scored_by_Neymar)) and assist provider (59) for the [Brazil national team](149286.md). He was recognised by [Opta Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opta_Sports) as one of the top two players in Europe for five consecutive seasons from 2015–16 to 2019–20, ranking as the best player in 2016–17 and 2017–18. He is one of the few players to have scored over 100 goals with three clubs.[^cite_note-4]

Neymar made his professional debut with Santos in 2009 and soon emerged as a teenage sensation. In [2011](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Santos_FC_season), he led Santos to the [Copa Libertadores](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Copa_Libertadores) title and was named the tournament's [best player](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Copa_Libertadores#Best_Player). Neymar joined [Barcelona](68187.md) in 2013, where he was part of the *[MSN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MSN_\(association_football\))* attacking trio alongside [Lionel Messi](2150841.md) and [Luis Suárez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luis_Su%C3%A1rez_\(Uruguayan_footballer\)). He finished as the joint [top scorer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_UEFA_Champions_League_top_scorers) in the [UEFA Champions League](44220.md) and the joint top scorer in the [Copa del Rey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Copa_del_Rey) as Barcelona completed the [continental treble](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Continental_treble) in [2014–15](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014%E2%80%9315_FC_Barcelona_season). At the age of 23, Neymar was viewed as one of the world's three best players, alongside Messi and [Cristiano Ronaldo](623737.md).&#x20;

In 2017, Neymar joined [Paris Saint-Germain](357488.md) in a [world-record](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most_expensive_association_football_transfers) €222 million transfer, however, his time at the club was affected by injuries. Despite this, he was particularly influential to [Ligue 1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ligue_1) title wins in [2017–18](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017%E2%80%9318_Ligue_1), [2019–20](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019%E2%80%9320_Ligue_1) and [2022–23](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022%E2%80%9323_Ligue_1), helping PSG become the [most decorated club in France](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_records_and_statistics_in_France#Total_titles_won_\(1918%E2%80%93present\)), but failed to lead them to their first Champions League title as they finished as runners-up in [2020](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020_UEFA_Champions_League_final). In [2020–21](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020%E2%80%9321_UEFA_Champions_League), Neymar became the first player to score 20 goals in the Champions League with two different clubs. With over 110 goals, he is PSG's [fourth-all-time top goalscorer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Paris_Saint-Germain_FC_records_and_statistics#Goalscorers). In 2023, he joined Saudi club [Al-Hilal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Hilal_SFC) before returning to Santos in January 2025.

Neymar was [capped](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cap_\(sport\)) 130 times for the Brazil national team, the [second-most](149286.md#Most_appearances) for the country. He appeared in four consecutive [FIFA World Cups](11370.md) from [2014](656933.md) to [2026](36581929.md), scoring [nine goals](8734046.md#Top_goalscorers) in 15 matches. His international honours include the [2013 FIFA Confederations Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_FIFA_Confederations_Cup), for which he won the [Golden Ball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Golden_Ball_\(FIFA_Confederations_Cup\)), as well as an [Olympic gold medal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olympic_medal) in [2016](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_at_the_2016_Summer_Olympics_%E2%80%93_Men's_tournament) — the nation's first — and a silver medal in [2012](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_at_the_2012_Summer_Olympics). After missing Brazil's triumph at the [2019 Copa América](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019_Copa_Am%C3%A9rica) due to injury, Neymar led them to the [final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Copa_Am%C3%A9rica_final) of the [2021 Copa América](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Copa_Am%C3%A9rica), for which he jointly received the [Golden Ball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Copa_Am%C3%A9rica#Awards). He retired from international football in 2026.

&#x20;One of the world's most famous athletes, Neymar has been named the world's most marketable athlete by *[SportsPro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SportsPro)*, has appeared regularly on *[Forbes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes)*' annual list of the [world's highest-paid athletes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Forbes_list_of_the_world's_highest-paid_athletes), and was included in the [*Time*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\)) [100 Most Influential People](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_100) list in 2017.

## Early life

Neymar was born in [Mogi das Cruzes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mogi_das_Cruzes), [São Paulo](229422.md), to Neymar Santos Sr. and Nadine da Silva and had a [Christian](18337522.md) upbringing.[^cite_note-neymarjr-5][^cite_note-instagram.com-6] He inherited his name from his father, who is a former footballer and became his son's advisor as Neymar's talents began to grow.[^cite_note-7] Neymar comments on his father's role: "My father has been by my side since I was little. He takes care of things, my finances and my family."[^cite_note-Samba_Star-8] Neymar has a very close [sibling relationship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sibling_relationship) with his sister Rafaella Beckran. He honoured Beckran by tattooing her face on his arm while she tattooed her brother's eyes on her arm.[^cite_note-9]

Growing up, Neymar combined his love of [futsal](188197.md) with [street football](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Street_football_\(association_football\)).[^cite_note-Back_Page-10] Neymar said that futsal had a massive influence on him growing up, helping him develop his technique, speed of thought and ability to perform moves in tight spaces.[^cite_note-11] He was inspired by [Robinho](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robinho) — his "idol" — [Ronaldinho](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ronaldinho), [Lionel Messi](2150841.md) and [Wayne Rooney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wayne_Rooney).[^cite_note-12][^cite_note-13][^cite_note-14]

In 2003, Neymar moved with his family to [São Vicente](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S%C3%A3o_Vicente,_S%C3%A3o_Paulo), where he began playing for youth side [Portuguesa Santista](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Associa%C3%A7%C3%A3o_Atl%C3%A9tica_Portuguesa_\(Santos\)).[^cite_note-15] Then, later in 2003, they moved to [Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santos,_S%C3%A3o_Paulo), where Neymar joined [Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santos_FC).[^cite_note-16] With the success of his youth career and added income, the family bought their first property, a house next to Vila Belmiro, Santos' home stadium. Their quality of family life improved, as at age 15, Neymar was earning 10000 [reais](34201323.md) per month and at 16, 125000 reais per month. At 17, he signed his first full professional contract, was upgraded to the Santos first team, and began signing his first [sponsorship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sponsor_\(commercial\)) deals.[^cite_note-Merca_Foot-17]

## Club career

### Santos

#### Youth

Neymar began playing football at an early age and he was soon spotted by [Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santos_FC) who offered him a contract in 2003; where he was inducted into their [youth academy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Football_academy), which has, in the past, produced Brazilian internationals like [Coutinho](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ant%C3%B4nio_Wilson_Vieira_Hon%C3%B3rio), [Clodoaldo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clodoaldo), [Diego](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diego_\(footballer,_born_1985\)), [Elano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elano) and [Alex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alex_\(footballer,_born_June_1982\)). He also joined the likes of [Pepe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jos%C3%A9_Macia), [Pelé](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pel%C3%A9) and Robinho in starting out his career at the club, nicknamed *Peixe*.[^cite_note-Santos_Player_Profile-18] While in the youth academy, Neymar met [Paulo Henrique Ganso](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paulo_Henrique_Ganso), becoming good friends in the process. Aged 14, Neymar travelled to Spain for tryouts with the [Real Madrid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Real_Madrid_C.F.) youth team. He did not stay in Madrid, however, as his father decided at the time that he preferred the young prodigy to keep growing up while playing at Santos.[^cite_note-19][^cite_note-20]

#### 2009–11: Teenage sensation and Copa Libertadores triumph

> "The 18-year-old is a magnificent prospect. He is sleek and skilful, able to beat the defender on either side, capable of combining well, and full of tricks he can put to productive use in and around the penalty area."

— South American football journalist [Tim Vickery](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tim_Vickery) on Neymar in 2010.

[^cite_note-21]

Neymar made his professional debut on 7 March 2009, one month after turning 17 years old, in a 2–1 win against [Oeste](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oeste_Futebol_Clube) in the [2009 Campeonato Paulista](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2009_Campeonato_Paulista).[^cite_note-Back_Page-10] On 11 April, Neymar scored the decisive goal in a 2–1 win against [Palmeiras](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sociedade_Esportiva_Palmeiras) in the [2009 Campeonato Paulista semi-final first leg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Campeonato_Paulista_2009#Semi-finals).[^cite_note-22] In the final, however, Santos suffered a 4–2 aggregate defeat to [Corinthians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sport_Club_Corinthians_Paulista).[^cite_note-23] Neymar continued his rapid development in 2010, scoring 42 goals in 60 matches in all competitions and helping Santos win its first ever [Copa do Brasil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Copa_do_Brasil) title, where he finished as the [tournament's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2010_Copa_do_Brasil) top scorer with 11 goals.[^cite_note-24][^cite_note-25][^cite_note-26] It was a breakout season for him, emerging as one of the hottest prospects in world football and being heralded the 'new Pelé'.[^cite_note-27][^cite_note-28][^cite_note-\:45-29] However, his apparent tendency for diving and his fallout with manager [Dorival Júnior](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dorival_J%C3%BAnior) drew criticism.[^cite_note-\:45-29][^cite_note-Back_Page-10]

In the 2010 summer transfer window, Santos received bids for Neymar from [Premier League](11250.md) clubs [Chelsea](7473.md) and [West Ham United](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Ham_United_F.C.), the former reported to be in the region of £20 million.[^cite_note-30][^cite_note-chelseachase-31] Neymar remained at Santos, but said a year later that it was a "dream" of his "to play in Europe", while also stating that at the time it had been the right decision to stay in Brazil.[^cite_note-chelseachase-31][^cite_note-32]



Neymar and [Lionel Messi](2150841.md) greeting each other after the [2011 FIFA Club World Cup final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_FIFA_Club_World_Cup_final)

Neymar scored crucial goals during the [2011 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Santos_FC_season), including the winning goal in the second leg of the finals against [Corinthians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SC_Corinthians_Paulista) to help his club clinch back-to-back state titles.[^cite_note-33] He scored six goals during Santos' run to the [2011 Copa Libertadores finals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Copa_Libertadores_finals), including the opening goal in the second leg of the final against Uruguayan side [Peñarol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/C.A._Pe%C3%B1arol), where Santos won 2–1. This was the club's first [Copa Libertadores](299730.md) title since the "Pelé era" in the 1960s.[^cite_note-34][^cite_note-35][^cite_note-36][^cite_note-37][^cite_note-38] Neymar was awarded Man of the Match and named the tournament's [Best Player](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_Copa_Libertadores#Best_Player).[^cite_note-39][^cite_note-40] On 27 July 2011, he scored a spectacular solo dribble in a thrilling 5–4 loss against Ronaldinho's [Flamengo](187239.md) that went on to be awarded the 2011 [FIFA Puskás Award](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_Pusk%C3%A1s_Award).[^cite_note-41] Later in the same year, he signed a contract extension until after the [2014 FIFA World Cup](656933.md), reportedly increasing his wages by 50%, shunning off interest from top European clubs, including [Real Madrid](26413.md).[^cite_note-42][^cite_note-43]

On 14 December, Neymar scored the opening goal from 20 meters for Santos as they defeated [Kashiwa Reysol](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kashiwa_Reysol) 3–1 in the semi-final of the [FIFA Club World Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_FIFA_Club_World_Cup) in [Toyota](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toyota,_Aichi), Japan.[^cite_note-44][^cite_note-45] In the final on 18 December against [Barcelona](68187.md), Neymar made a very small impact and Santos were defeated 4–0.[^cite_note-46][^cite_note-\:63-47] He was awarded the tournament's [Bronze Ball](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_Club_World_Cup_awards#Golden_Ball) as the third-best player, behind Messi and [Xavi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xavi_\(footballer,_born_1980\)).[^cite_note-48] On 31 December, he won the [2011 South American Footballer of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_South_American_Footballer_of_the_Year) award for the first time, by a record margin.[^cite_note-49] In a [UOL](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Universo_Online) survey of 100 [Campeonato Brasileiro Série A](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Campeonato_Brasileiro_S%C3%A9rie_A) players, Neymar was voted the best player in Brazil, earning 89% of the votes.[^cite_note-50]

#### 2012–13: Continued individual dominance and final season



Neymar (pictured in 2012) was awarded the [South American Footballer of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_South_American_Footballer_of_the_Year) for the second consecutive time.

The [2012 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Santos_FC_season) was arguably Neymar's finest with Santos. Early in the year, at 19 years old, Neymar finished 10th in the [2011 FIFA Ballon d'Or](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2011_FIFA_Ballon_d'Or) rankings. This was an unprecedented feat for a non-European club player.[^cite_note-51] On 5 February 2012, when he turned 20, he scored his 100th goal as a professional football player, against Palmeiras in the [Campeonato Paulista](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Campeonato_Paulista).[^cite_note-52][^cite_note-53] On 25 February, he scored two goals, one of which was from 25-yards, and created two assists to help his side to a 6–1 win over [Ponte Preta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Associa%C3%A7%C3%A3o_Atl%C3%A9tica_Ponte_Preta).[^cite_note-54] On 7 March, Neymar netted a [hat-trick](411541.md) as Santos saw off Brazilian rivals [Internacional](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sport_Club_Internacional) with a 3–1 win in the [Copa Libertadores](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Copa_Libertadores) group stage.[^cite_note-55] In the fixture against [São Paulo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S%C3%A3o_Paulo_FC) on 29 April, he scored a hat-trick with the match ending 3–1.[^cite_note-56]

In the [2012 Campeonato Paulista finals](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Campeonato_Paulista_knockout_phase#Finals) against [Guarani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guarani_FC), Neymar scored twice in the [first](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Campeonato_Paulista_knockout_phase#First_leg) and [second legs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Campeonato_Paulista_knockout_phase#Second_leg), which ended 7–2 on aggregate to crown Santos champions.[^cite_note-57][^cite_note-58] He finished the [2012 Campeonato Paulista](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Campeonato_Paulista) with 20 goals and was voted the Best Player and Best Forward. He also finished as joint top scorer in the [Copa Libertadores](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Copa_Libertadores) with eight goals, where Santos were eliminated by eventual champions Corinthians in the semi-finals. He was voted the Best Player of the [2012 Recopa Sudamericana](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Recopa_Sudamericana), which Santos won.



Neymar's autographed boots from the [2013 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_Santos_FC_season) on display at an exhibition&#x20;

On 25 August 2012, he scored a brace in the 2–1 away win at [Palmeiras](187243.md).[^cite_note-59] On 3 November, in the [league](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_Campeonato_Brasileiro_S%C3%A9rie_A) fixture away at [Cruzeiro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cruzeiro_Esporte_Clube), Neymar scored a hat-trick and assisted [Felipe Anderson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Felipe_Anderson)'s goal, to help his side to a 4–0 win.[^cite_note-60] Neymar finished off the 2012 season in style, first setting-up [Victor Andrade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victor_Andrade)'s equaliser, then scoring twice, to give Santos a 3–1 home win over Palmeiras on 1 December.[^cite_note-61] Neymar finished the 2012 season with 43 goals in just 47 matches. He won the [2012 South American Footballer of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012_South_American_Footballer_of_the_Year), retaining his award.[^cite_note-62] Pelé claimed that Neymar is the best player in the world; former Argentine player [Diego Maradona](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Diego_Maradona) responded to this: "Maybe Neymar is the best player in the world, but only if you say that Messi is from a different planet."[^cite_note-63] At this point in his career, Neymar was touted to emulate Messi — or even surpass him.[^cite_note-64][^cite_note-65][^cite_note-66]

Neymar started the [2013 Campeonato Paulista](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_Campeonato_Paulista) scoring twice in the first match, which ended in a 3–1 win over [São Bernardo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S%C3%A3o_Bernardo_Futebol_Clube) on 19 January 2013.[^cite_note-67] On 3 February, in the Paulista fixture against [São Paulo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S%C3%A3o_Paulo_FC), Santos won 3–1 with Neymar scoring and making two assists.[^cite_note-68] Often compared to Pelé early in his career, he appeared on the cover of *[Time](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Time_\(magazine\))* magazine with the title 'The Next Pelé' in February 2013.[^cite_note-69][^cite_note-70] He scored all four goals as Santos beat [União Barbarense](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uni%C3%A3o_Agr%C3%ADcola_Barbarense_Futebol_Clube) 4–0 in the [Paulista](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paulista) on 13 April.[^cite_note-71] On 25 April, his agent and father revealed that Neymar intended to leave for Europe before the 2014 World Cup.[^cite_note-72] Neymar said the month prior that he had a "dream of playing in Europe, for a big club like Barcelona, Real Madrid and Chelsea."[^cite_note-73] Ahead of his last match for Santos, on 26 May against [Flamengo](187239.md) in the first round of the [2013 Brazilian Championship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_Campeonato_Brasileiro_S%C3%A9rie_A), Neymar was in tears during the national anthem, with the match ending in a draw.[^cite_note-74]

In 2021, UOL included Neymar in their ranking of the 'Top 5 Greatest Players in the History of Santos'.[^cite_note-75]

### Barcelona

Main article: [Neymargate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neymargate)



Neymar during his presentation as a [Barcelona](68187.md) player on 3 June 2013

On 24 May 2013, Santos announced that they had received two offers for Neymar. The following day, Neymar announced he would sign with Barcelona on 27 May and join the team after playing in the [2013 FIFA Confederations Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_FIFA_Confederations_Cup). Neither Neymar nor the clubs released details on the transfer fee or personal terms, save to say he signed a five-year deal.[^cite_note-76] On 3 June, Neymar was unveiled by Barcelona after passing medical tests and signing a contract that would keep him at the club through June 2018.[^cite_note-77]

Neymar was presented at the [Camp Nou](222488.md) in front of 56,500 fans, a record turnout for a Brazilian player.[^cite_note-78] Club vice-president [Josep Maria Bartomeu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Josep_Maria_Bartomeu) initially said Neymar's transfer fee was €57.1 million and his release clause set at €190 million.[^cite_note-79][^cite_note-80] Barcelona's doctor suggested he might need to gain weight to be able to cope physically in Spanish football.[^cite_note-81]

#### Transfer investigation

In January 2014, the prosecutor's office in [Madrid](41188263.md) began investigating the transfer fee that Barcelona paid for Neymar.[^cite_note-Staatsanwaltschaft_untersucht_Transfer_von_Neymar-82] The documents submitted to the authorities on request contained contradictory information.[^cite_note-Staatsanwaltschaft_untersucht_Transfer_von_Neymar-82] On 23 January 2014, [Sandro Rosell](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sandro_Rosell) resigned from his position as president.[^cite_note-83] A day later, the details of the transfer were revealed by Barcelona; the transfer had in fact cost them €86.2 million (£71.5 million),[^cite_note-BBC_Real-84][^cite_note-SkyReal-85][^cite_note-86] with Neymar's parents confirmed to have received a €40 million sum.[^cite_note-87][^cite_note-88] In the aftermath, Barcelona and Bartomeu were charged with [tax fraud](2256795.md).[^cite_note-89]

#### 2013–14: Settling into Spain



Neymar playing in a [La Liga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Liga) fixture against [Almería](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UD_Almer%C3%ADa) in March 2014

Neymar made his official debut for Barcelona during the opening game of the [2013–14](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013%E2%80%9314_FC_Barcelona_season) [La Liga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013%E2%80%9314_La_Liga) season as a 63rd-minute substitute for [Alexis Sánchez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alexis_S%C3%A1nchez) in a 7–0 win against [Levante](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Levante_UD).[^cite_note-90] On 21 August, he played in the [2013 Supercopa de España](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013_Supercopa_de_Espa%C3%B1a) against [Copa del Rey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2012%E2%80%9313_Copa_del_Rey) holders [Atlético Madrid](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atl%C3%A9tico_Madrid); he made an immediate impact, scoring Barcelona's only goal as they won the Supercopa on away goals.[^cite_note-91]

On 18 September, he made his [UEFA Champions League](44220.md) debut, [assisting](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assist_\(football\)) a [Gerard Piqué](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gerard_Piqu%C3%A9) goal as Barça beat [Ajax](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AFC_Ajax) 4–0 in their opening match of the [2013–14 tournament](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013%E2%80%9314_UEFA_Champions_League). Six days later, Neymar scored his first goal in La Liga in Barcelona's 4–1 defeat of [Real Sociedad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Real_Sociedad) at Camp Nou.[^cite_note-92] On 26 October, in his first *[El Clásico](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/El_Cl%C3%A1sico)* appearance, he scored the opening goal and assisted the team's winning goal in a 2–1 win over Real Madrid.[^cite_note-clasico-93] On 11 December, he scored a [hat-trick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_UEFA_Champions_League_hat-tricks) in a 6–1 win over [Celtic](69708.md) in the [group stage](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2013%E2%80%9314_UEFA_Champions_League_group_stage#Group_H).[^cite_note-94] A few days later, on 14 December, Neymar scored both of Barcelona's goals in their 2–1 win against [Villarreal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Villarreal_CF).[^cite_note-95]

15 games into the La Liga season, Neymar had produced a respectable four goals and eight assists, nailing down the left-wing position in the Barcelona team.[^cite_note-\:41-96] However, Neymar felt low, expressing in an interview over a decade later: "I spent the first 6 months at Barça with butterflies in my stomach".[^cite_note-\:41-96] On 26 March 2014, Neymar scored twice in a 3–0 win against [Celta Vigo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RC_Celta_de_Vigo).[^cite_note-97] On 1 April, he scored in the Champions League quarter-final first leg against Atlético Madrid to level the match 1–1, though Atlético defeated Barcelona 1–0 in the second leg to eliminate them.[^cite_note-98] On 16 April, Neymar played in the [2014 Copa del Rey final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014_Copa_del_Rey_final) against Real Madrid, which Barcelona lost.[^cite_note-99] He featured heavily in his debut season with Barcelona, ending the campaign with 15 goals in 41 appearances.[^cite_note-100]

#### 2014–15: *MSN* trio and continental treble

&#x20;The [2014–15 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014%E2%80%9315_FC_Barcelona_season) notably marked the birth of the new attacking trio of [Lionel Messi](2150841.md), new signing [Luis Suárez](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Luis_Su%C3%A1rez_\(Uruguayan_footballer\)) and Neymar — dubbed *[MSN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MSN_\(association_football\))*. A sports writer noted that Neymar's understanding with Messi, in comparison to the previous season, was "close to telepathic at times", and the addition of Suárez's "tenacity and hunger" resulted in "possibly the most formidable strike-force in European football".[^cite_note-UEFAErrors-101]



Neymar during a La Liga match against [Villarreal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Villarreal_CF) in February 2015

On 13 September, Neymar scored both of Barcelona's goals in their 2–0 win against [Athletic Bilbao](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Athletic_Bilbao) in [La Liga](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014%E2%80%9315_La_Liga).[^cite_note-102] Two weeks later, he scored a hat-trick against [Granada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Granada_CF) in a 6–0 win on 27 September.[^cite_note-103] On 24 January 2015, Neymar scored twice and assisted a goal in a 6–0 win at [Elche](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elche_CF).[^cite_note-auto-104] He continued to deliver for Barcelona, scoring twice in the [Copa del Rey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014%E2%80%9315_Copa_del_Rey) round of 16, twice in the quarter-final and twice in the semi-final to reach the [final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_Copa_del_Rey_Final).[^cite_note-105][^cite_note-106][^cite_note-107] After defeating Atlético Madrid 1–0 to secure the league title on 17 May, Barcelona defeated Athletic Bilbao 3–1 at Camp Nou in the [2015 Copa del Rey final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_Copa_del_Rey_final) on 30 May, with Neymar scoring the club's second goal to tie him as the competition's [top goalscorer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014%E2%80%9315_Copa_del_Rey#Top_goalscorers) with 7.[^cite_note-108][^cite_note-109]

Neymar was vital for Barcelona in the [Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2014%E2%80%9315_UEFA_Champions_League), scoring six goals in the knock out stages against [Paris Saint-Germain](357488.md) (PSG) and [Bayern Munich](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FC_Bayern_Munich) as Barcelona proceeded to the [2015 Champions League final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_UEFA_Champions_League_Final) on 6 June.[^cite_note-110][^cite_note-111][^cite_note-112] In the final, Neymar scored the third goal for Barcelona in the 3–1 defeat of Italian champions [Juventus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juventus_FC) at Berlin's [Olympiastadion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olympiastadion_\(Berlin\)), ensuring the club won its fifth Champions League title and become the first club in history to win the [treble](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Treble_\(association_football\)) twice.[^cite_note-triple-113] Neymar became one of the few players to win both the [Copa Libertadores](299730.md) and the Champions League,[^cite_note-114] and the first player to score in both quarter-final matches, both semi-final matches and the final of the Champions League.[^cite_note-115]

Neymar ended the season with 39 goals in all competitions, including 10 in the Champions League, making him the joint top scorer with Ronaldo and teammate Messi in the latter competition.[^cite_note-116][^cite_note-\:43-117][^cite_note-118] He was the first player apart from those two to top the competition's scoring list since compatriot [Kaká](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kak%C3%A1) in [2006–07](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2006%E2%80%9307_UEFA_Champions_League).[^cite_note-119] Barcelona's attacking trio, *MSN*, ended the season with a combined 122 goals, the most in a season for an attacking trio in Spanish football history.[^cite_note-120] *[France Football](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/France_Football)* ranked Neymar as the best under-23 player in the world and included him in their World XI.[^cite_note-121][^cite_note-122]

#### 2015–17: Ballon d'Or candidate, domestic double and final season

&#x20;In the [2015–16 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015%E2%80%9316_FC_Barcelona_season), on 12 September, Neymar scored a near-immediate equaliser against Atlético Madrid in La Liga before Barcelona went on to win the match 2–1.[^cite_note-123] In late September, Messi suffered a serious knee injury in the league, keeping him out for two months.[^cite_note-124] In Messi's absence, Neymar experienced one of the greatest peaks in his career, scoring 13 goals and providing 9 assists in just 8 games.[^cite_note-\:19-125][^cite_note-126] His run included a four goal display in Barcelona's 5–2 home win in the league over [Rayo Vallecano](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rayo_Vallecano);[^cite_note-127] a two goal display in a 3–0 win against [BATE Borisov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FC_BATE_Borisov) in the [Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015%E2%80%9316_UEFA_Champions_League) groupstage;[^cite_note-128] two goals in a 3–0 win against Villarreal, including a "stunning" goal where he received the ball from just inside the area and flicked it up over his shoulder and over a defender before volleying it into the goal;[^cite_note-129] two goals in a 4–0 victory against Real Sociedad; and a goal alongside an assist in Barcelona's 4–0 away win against Real Madrid.[^cite_note-130] Villarreal manager [Marcelino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marcelino_Garc%C3%ADa_Toral) lauded Neymar as "probably the most in-form attacker in the league, decisive in front of goal and also in their \[buildup] play".[^cite_note-\:19-125] Neymar was named the [La Liga Player of the Month](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Liga_Player_of_the_Month) for November, which made him the first Barcelona player to receive the award since it was inaugurated in September 2013.[^cite_note-131] In December, he featured in the [2015 FIFA Club World Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_FIFA_Club_World_Cup), delivering two assists in Barcelona's 3–0 win against [River Plate](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Club_Atl%C3%A9tico_River_Plate) in the [final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_FIFA_Club_World_Cup_final) on 20 December.[^cite_note-132]&#x20;



Neymar training with the [Brazil under-23](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brazil_national_under-23_football_team) in August 2016, soon after his contract extension with Barcelona

At 23 years old, Neymar was being praised for elevating to the same level of Messi and Ronaldo, possibly entering the best player in the world debate.[^cite_note-\:15-133][^cite_note-134][^cite_note-135] He was shortlisted for the [2015 FIFA Ballon d'Or](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015_FIFA_Ballon_d'Or) alongside Messi and Ronaldo. Several journalists argued that he deserved to win the award, particularly for his crucial performances in the knockout stages of the 2014–15 Champions League and during Messi's absence during the first half of 2015–16.[^cite_note-136][^cite_note-137][^cite_note-138] He came in third place, receiving 7.86 percent of the vote.[^cite_note-FIFA_2015-139] Both *[L'Équipe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/L'%C3%89quipe)* and *[Sky Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sky_Sports)* ranked Neymar as the second-best player in the world of 2015, only behind Messi.[^cite_note-\:53-140][^cite_note-\:60-141]

In the quarter-final of the [Copa del Rey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2015%E2%80%9316_Copa_del_Rey) in January 2016, Neymar scored twice in Barcelona's 5–2 aggregate win over Athletic Bilbao.[^cite_note-142][^cite_note-143] On 12 March, in a 6–0 win, he scored twice against [Getafe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Getafe_CF) in the league.[^cite_note-144] Neymar had another successful La Liga campaign, being directly involved in 40 goals (24 goals and 16 assists) as Barcelona won the title, finishing just one point ahead of Real Madrid.[^cite_note-\:69-145] He also won eight penalties for Barcelona, more than any other player in the league.[^cite_note-\:69-145] He was included in *[The Guardian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Guardian)*'s La Liga Team of the Season.[^cite_note-146] On 22 May, he scored in Barcelona's 2–0 win against [Sevilla](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sevilla_FC) in the [2016 Copa del Rey final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_Copa_del_Rey_final), celebrating a [domestic double](88184.md#Spain) triumph.[^cite_note-147][^cite_note-148]

The front three of Messi, Suárez and Neymar finished the season with 131 goals, breaking the record they had set the previous year for most goals by an attacking trio in a single season.[^cite_note-149] Despite a dip in form at the end of the season, Neymar ultimately reached over 30 goals for the second consecutive season, scoring 31, as well as 27 assists.[^cite_note-150][^cite_note-151] For the second consecutive year, *Sky Sports* ranked him at number two in their 2016 Top 100 Players of the Year list.[^cite_note-152]

On 1 July 2016, Neymar signed a new contract with Barcelona until 2021. His new contract included a €200 million buy-out clause, rising to €222m in the second year of his deal, and €250m thereafter. His future at Barcelona had been in doubt over rumours that the likes of PSG, Real Madrid and [Manchester City](165813.md) were considering to make huge offers for him.[^cite_note-153]

&#x20;On 12 September of the [2016–17 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016%E2%80%9317_Paris_Saint-Germain_FC_season), Neymar registered a goal and four assists in Barcelona's 7–0 win against Celtic in the group stage of the [Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016%E2%80%9317_UEFA_Champions_League).[^cite_note-154] In an away fixture in La Liga against [Sporting Gijón](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sporting_de_Gij%C3%B3n) on 24 September, he scored twice in a 5–0 win.[^cite_note-155] On 19 January 2017, he scored his club's only goal in a 1–0 win in the first leg of the [Copa del Rey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016%E2%80%9317_Copa_del_Rey) quarter-final against Real Sociedad, converting from the penalty spot.[^cite_note-156] Neymar's form drastically dipped, having scored just five goals in La Liga by February.[^cite_note-\:49-157] [ESPN](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ESPN) said that Neymar looked "rusty in front of goal".[^cite_note-\:49-157] While tiredness was cited as an explanation, sections of the media instead attributed his decline to the aggressive treatment he received from oppositions, with *[Mundo Deportivo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mundo_Deportivo)* describing him as "the most hunted player in La Liga".[^cite_note-\:49-157]&#x20;



Neymar celebrating after Barcelona's sixth goal against [PSG](357488.md) in the [UEFA Champions League](44220.md) second leg win in March 2017

Neymar was pivotal in Barcelona's [6–1 victory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barcelona_6%E2%80%931_Paris_Saint-Germain) (widely known as *La Remontada* English translation: *The Comeback*) over PSG in the Champions League round of 16 on 8 March, overcoming the 4–0 loss in the first leg.[^cite_note-158] It is widely regarded as the greatest comeback in football history.[^cite_note-159][^cite_note-160][^cite_note-161] Barcelona were 5–3 down on aggregate in the 88th minute, but scored three goals in the final seven minutes to progress into the quarter-final — Neymar contributed to all three goals, scoring twice and providing an assist in the 95th minute.[^cite_note-\:37-162][^cite_note-163] A sports writer said that Neymar's performance cemented his legacy as the third-best player of his generation, behind [Messi and Ronaldo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Messi%E2%80%93Ronaldo_rivalry).[^cite_note-\:14-164] PSG's midfielder [Marco Verratti](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marco_Verratti) later said, "If I could sign one player for PSG it would be Neymar \[...] He is at the same level as Messi."[^cite_note-\:66-165]

On 15 May, Neymar scored his first away hat-trick for Barcelona in a 4–1 victory against [Las Palmas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UD_Las_Palmas), ensuring that Barcelona were still in the La Liga title race, but Real Madrid ultimately won the title on 20 May.[^cite_note-166][^cite_note-\:29-167] On 27 May, he scored his 105th, and final, goal for Barcelona in the [2017 Copa del Rey final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017_Copa_del_Rey_Final), as Barcelona defeated [Alavés](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deportivo_Alav%C3%A9s) 3–1.[^cite_note-168] This made him the first player to score in three consecutive Copa del Rey finals since [Ferenc Puskás](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ferenc_Pusk%C3%A1s) in the 1960s.[^cite_note-169] Despite being less prolific than his previous two seasons, prominent journalist [Guillem Balagué](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guillem_Balagu%C3%A9) credited Neymar for being more of a provider and for maturing his game.[^cite_note-\:61-170] In Balagué's view, Neymar was "now second only to Messi as the best player in the world."[^cite_note-\:61-170] According to [*WhoScored*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opta_Sports#Association_football), which is powered by [Opta Sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Opta_Sports) data, Neymar was Europe's best-performing player during the season.[^cite_note-\:42-171]

By July 2017, media speculation emerged that Neymar wanted to leave Barcelona for PSG, who he had been "close to joining" the previous year according to his agent Wagner Ribeiro.[^cite_note-172] Neymar was driven to join PSG as he wanted a new challenge and to play with several of his Brazil national team teammates who were at the club, including new signing [Dani Alves](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dani_Alves).[^cite_note-173][^cite_note-174][^cite_note-175] On 2 August, Neymar asked Barcelona to allow him to leave.[^cite_note-176] Barcelona president, Bartomeu, admitted he was powerless to stop him from wanting to leave.[^cite_note-177] In the short time that Neymar was at Barcelona, he was a "crucial piece" to the club's success in those years, scoring 105 goals and assisting 76 in 186 appearances.[^cite_note-178][^cite_note-\:8-179][^cite_note-180] *[Sports Illustrated](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sports_Illustrated)* said that he arguably eclipsed Ronaldo as the second-best player in the world during his final three seasons at Barcelona.[^cite_note-181]

### Paris Saint-Germain



Neymar posing with his PSG number 10 jersey alongside club president [Nasser Al-Khelaifi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nasser_Al-Khelaifi)

On 3 August 2017, Barcelona announced that Neymar's legal representatives made a payment of €222 million to the club, equal to the [release clause](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Release_clause) of his contract, which constituted the [most expensive transfer ever](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_most_expensive_association_football_transfers).[^cite_note-182][^cite_note-BBCTransfer2017-183] The situation was unusual, in that the fee was paid to the club directly, after La Liga had refused to receive the payment — citing violation of [Financial Fair Play](34255206.md) (FFP) rules by PSG.[^cite_note-184][^cite_note-185]

Neymar's unexpected departure from Barcelona and the record-breaking transfer fee made global headlines, with some in the media labelling it as "the signing of the century".[^cite_note-186][^cite_note-auto1-187] He joined PSG on a contract that would run until 2022, earning roughly €36 million a year — one of the [largest sports contracts](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_largest_sports_contracts) — to make him the second-highest paid player in the world.[^cite_note-188][^cite_note-189] This was a €12 million increase from his salary at Barcelona.[^cite_note-190]

As the club's main player, Neymar was touted to lead PSG to their first Champions League title.[^cite_note-auto1-187][^cite_note-191][^cite_note-192][^cite_note-193] He was given the number 10 shirt by the club's midfielder [Javier Pastore](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javier_Pastore) as a "welcome gift".[^cite_note-194][^cite_note-195] The [Eiffel Tower](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eiffel_Tower) celebrated the arrival of Neymar by displaying his name and the red and blue colours of PSG.[^cite_note-196]

The relationship between Neymar and Barcelona fans was tainted following his move to PSG.[^cite_note-\:8-179] In his presentation on 17 August, he said that leaving Barcelona was one of the toughest decisions in his life.[^cite_note-197] He joined a growing list of Brazilian players who had signed for PSG in previous years, including [Thiago Silva](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Thiago_Silva), [Lucas Moura](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lucas_Moura) and [David Luiz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Luiz).[^cite_note-198][^cite_note-199]

#### Contract breach lawsuit

On 27 August 2017, Barcelona filed a lawsuit against Neymar, demanding he return the contract-renewal bonus he'd received as well as €8.5 million in [damages](8134.md) and an additional 10% in [arrears](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arrears). The club claimed the money is owed on the basis of the renewal of the contract they signed in 2016. The club also requested PSG to take on the financial responsibility for the payment of the fees if the player is shown unable to do so.[^cite_note-200] Neymar's lawyers announced that they would contest the case,[^cite_note-201] but on 19 June 2020, a Spanish court ruled that Neymar must return \$7.5 million (€6.7 million) to Barcelona.[^cite_note-202][^cite_note-203] In 2021, the club and the player announced they've reached an "amicable" [out-of-court settlement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Out-of-court_settlement), whose details were not publicly divulged.[^cite_note-204]

#### 2017–19: PSG's talisman, domestic success and injuries



Neymar ahead of a [Ligue 1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ligue_1) fixture against [Toulouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toulouse_FC) in February 2018

Neymar made his debut for PSG on 13 August 2017, scoring a goal and assisting another in a 3–0 away victory over [Guingamp](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/En_Avant_de_Guingamp) in the league.[^cite_note-205] Guingamp's defender [Lucas Deaux](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lucas_Deaux) said "I have never seen a player like this before".[^cite_note-206] In the next fixture on 20 August against [Toulouse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toulouse_FC), Neymar contributed with two goals and two assists to help PSG win 6–2.[^cite_note-207] On 30 September, Neymar scored twice in another 6–2 win as PSG defeated [Bordeaux](1135285.md).[^cite_note-208] On 22 October, he scored against rivals [Marseille](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olympique_de_Marseille) in his first *[Le Classique](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Classique)* in a 2–2 result, but was sent off — the first time in his PSG career.[^cite_note-209] On 16 December, Neymar scored twice and assisted twice to help PSG defeat [Rennes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stade_Rennais_FC) 4–1.[^cite_note-210] As the talisman at PSG, Neymar had immediately established himself as the club's most influential player, enjoying more freedom than he had at Barcelona. [^cite_note-211][^cite_note-ESPNPSG2017-212][^cite_note-BBCPrince2025-213]

In the [Champions League group stage](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017%E2%80%9318_UEFA_Champions_League_group_stage), Neymar scored six goals in six games against Celtic, Bayern Munich and [Anderlecht](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RSC_Anderlecht) as PSG topped their group table.[^cite_note-214] The club had also set a new record for most goals in the group stage, with 25 goals.[^cite_note-\:55-215] 16 of the club's goals were scored by the new attacking trio of Neymar, [Edinson Cavani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Edinson_Cavani) and [Kylian Mbappé](48711857.md), turning PSG into a serious contender in the competition.[^cite_note-216][^cite_note-ESPNPSG2017-212][^cite_note-BBCPrince2025-213] In December, the [CIES Football Observatory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CIES_Football_Observatory) produced a team of the best performing players in Europe since the start of the season; Neymar was included alongside his fellow PSG teammates Verratti and Alves.[^cite_note-217] Neymar was also included in the [FIFA FIFPRO World 11](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFPRO#FIFA_FIFPRO_Men's_World_11) for the second time in his career.[^cite_note-\:67-218]

On 7 January 2018, Neymar scored twice in his first [Coupe de France](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coupe_de_France) match as his side defeated Rennes 6–1 to progress to the round of 32.[^cite_note-219] On 17 January, he displayed one of his greatest career performances, scoring four goals and assisting two in an 8–0 thrashing of [Dijon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dijon_FCO) in the league, including an excellent solo goal starting from the byline.[^cite_note-220][^cite_note-\:32-221] He became one of the few players to earn a 10 rating from *L'Équipe*.[^cite_note-\:32-221] By the end of January, Neymar was considered the most in-form player in Europe, having been involved in 40 of PSG's goals (24 goals and 16 assists).[^cite_note-222] On 14 February, he featured in his first Champions League knock out game for PSG, delivering a "clever" back-heel pass to assist the opening goal against Real Madrid in the round of 16 first leg, though they went on to lose 3–1.[^cite_note-223]

Neymar's [2017–18 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2017%E2%80%9318_Paris_Saint-Germain_FC_season) ended in late February 2018 after suffering a [metatarsal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fifth_metatarsal_bone) injury in his right foot in a league fixture against Marseille, causing him to miss PSG's crucial second leg tie against Real Madrid in the Champions League, who ultimately eliminated them.[^cite_note-224] He scored 28 goals in 30 matches, helping his new club win the [domestic treble](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Domestic_treble) of the Ligue 1, Coupe de France, and [Coupe de la Ligue](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Coupe_de_la_Ligue).[^cite_note-\:39-225][^cite_note-226][^cite_note-227] He was particularly the driving force to PSG retrieving the Ligue 1 title following [Monaco](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AS_Monaco_FC)'s triumph in the previous season, being rewarded with the [Ligue 1 Player of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ligue_1_Player_of_the_Year).[^cite_note-228][^cite_note-229][^cite_note-\:33-230]

&#x20;Neymar was included in the [ESM Team of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Sports_Magazines#ESM_Team_of_the_Year) for the first time,[^cite_note-\:36-231] and for the second consecutive season was recognised as the best-performing player in Europe according to *WhoScored*, recording the highest ever rating for a single season in one of Europe's top five leagues up to that point.[^cite_note-WhoScoredPSG-232] *WhoScored* also included him in their European Team of the Season for the third consecutive season.[^cite_note-\:69-145][^cite_note-\:42-171][^cite_note-233] Despite his success with PSG, Neymar finished outside of the top 10 for the [2018 Ballon d'Or](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018_Ballon_d'Or) (12th) for the first time since 2012, with journalists citing his cut short season with PSG and his disappointing [2018 FIFA World Cup](13327177.md) tournament with Brazil as the reason for the low place finish.[^cite_note-234]



Neymar before the Champions League fixture against [Red Star Belgrade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Star_Belgrade) in December 2018

On 12 August 2018, Neymar scored PSG's opening league goal of the [2018–19 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018%E2%80%9319_Paris_Saint-Germain_F.C._season) in a 3–0 home win over [Caen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stade_Malherbe_Caen).[^cite_note-235] On 29 September, he scored twice in the league fixture against [Nice](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OGC_Nice), winning 3–0.[^cite_note-236] He scored what proved to be the winning goal in a 2–1 victory over [Lille](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lille_OSC) on 2 November after Lille pulled one back with a late penalty.[^cite_note-237] This was PSG's 12th consecutive win since the start of the Ligue 1 season, an all-time record for any team in the [big five](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Big_Five_\(association_football\)) European leagues at the time.[^cite_note-238]

In the [Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018%E2%80%9319_UEFA_Champions_League) group stage, Neymar helped PSG top a group that included the previous year's runners-up [Liverpool](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liverpool_F.C.), as well as [Napoli](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SSC_Napoli) and [Red Star Belgrade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Red_Star_Belgrade).[^cite_note-UEFAErrors-101] He scored a hattrick in a 6–1 win over Red Star Belgrade (including two free kicks) and PSG's second goal in their 2–1 win against Liverpool, which meant he surpassed Kaká's tally of 30 goals to become Brazil's all-time top scorer in the Champions League with 31 goals.[^cite_note-239][^cite_note-240][^cite_note-\:25-241] For the second consecutive season, *[Goal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Goal_\(website\))* included Neymar in their Champions League Team of the Group Stage.[^cite_note-242][^cite_note-\:21-243]

On 19 January 2019, Neymar provided two goals and an assist in PSG's 9–0 defeat of Guingamp, the club's biggest home win.[^cite_note-244][^cite_note-245] On 23 January, nearly a year after his season-ending injury, Neymar reinjured the metatarsal in his right foot in PSG's win against [Strasbourg](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RC_Strasbourg_Alsace) in the Coupe de France.[^cite_note-246] He missed the Champions League round of 16 tie against [Manchester United](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Manchester_United_F.C.); after United eliminated PSG, Neymar went on social media to insult video review officials for awarding a stoppage-time penalty to United, resulting in [UEFA](32332.md) banning him for three matches in UEFA competitions for the insult.[^cite_note-APgoad-247][^cite_note-248]

On 27 April, Neymar returned from the foot injury to play in the [2019 Coupe de France final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019_Coupe_de_France_final) against Rennes, where he assisted the opening goal and scored PSG's second, but Rennes made a comeback and won on penalties. After the match, he was caught on video appearing to punch a spectator in the face, who had been filming and insulting PSG players.[^cite_note-APgoad-247][^cite_note-goalpunch2-249] Although PSG had already clinched the [Ligue 1](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2018%E2%80%9319_Ligue_1) title on 21 April, Neymar continued to contribute in the league.[^cite_note-250] On 4 May, he scored the equaliser from the penalty spot in a 1–1 draw with Nice.[^cite_note-251] On the next matchday, 11 May, he opened the scoring with a diving header before assisting PSG's second goal in a 2–1 win against [Angers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angers_SCO).[^cite_note-252] Neymar was banned for three matches for his actions in the Coupe de France final, causing him to miss the final two league games of the season and the eventual [2019 Trophée des Champions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019_Troph%C3%A9e_des_Champions).[^cite_note-253]

Despite the cup final disappointment and another injury interrupted season, Neymar trailed only Mbappé in PSG's top goalscorers of the season, with 23 goals in 28 matches.[^cite_note-254][^cite_note-255][^cite_note-goalpunch2-249] However, he was not included on the 30-man shortlist for the [Ballon d'Or](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019_Ballon_d'Or) for the first time since 2010 due to having a "black year": injuries, including an additional injury in the summer that caused him to miss Brazil's triumph at the [2019 Copa América](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019_Copa_Am%C3%A9rica), and the controversies off the field.[^cite_note-\:35-256] Nevertheless, at the end of the year *France Football* included him in their Team of the Decade for 2011–2020.[^cite_note-\:46-257]

#### 2019–20: Decisive in Ligue 1, Coupe de France final winning goal and UEFA Champions League runner-up

&#x20;In July 2019, it was revealed that Neymar wanted to leave PSG as he felt referees never protected him, there was jealousy towards him from some dressing room team-mates, fans did not appreciate his talent showcased in PSG and sections of the French media were against him.[^cite_note-\:9-258] He heavily pushed for a move back to Barcelona, and was also linked with Real Madrid.[^cite_note-\:10-259][^cite_note-260] He ultimately agreed to stay at PSG after talks with Barcelona broke down.[^cite_note-\:10-259][^cite_note-\:9-258]&#x20;



Neymar before converting his penalty against [Lille](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lille_OSC) in Ligue 1 in January 2020

The long drawn-out transfer speculation of Neymar returning to Barcelona caused tensions in Paris.[^cite_note-\:34-261] On 14 September, Neymar's first appearance for PSG in the [2019–20 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019%E2%80%9320_Paris_Saint-Germain_F.C._season), he was relentlessly booed throughout the match before scoring a [bicycle kick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bicycle_kick) in the 92nd minute to win 1–0 against Strasbourg in Ligue 1.[^cite_note-\:34-261][^cite_note-262] He scored another late winner on the next matchday as PSG defeated [Lyon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olympique_Lyonnais) 1–0 on 23 September.[^cite_note-263] Neymar continued to be vital in Ligue 1, scoring the only goal in a win against Bordeaux,[^cite_note-264] scoring and assisting in a comeback win against [Montpellier](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Montpellier_HSC),[^cite_note-\:27-265] assisting twice and scoring in a 4–0 win against [Saint-Étienne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AS_Saint-%C3%89tienne),[^cite_note-266] and scoring both goals in a 2–0 win against Lille.[^cite_note-267]

Neymar notably went on a seven-match scoring run and matched the record of Marseille's [Gunnar Andersson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gunnar_Andersson_\(footballer\)) in the early 1950s of scoring 47 goals in his first 50 matches in Ligue 1.[^cite_note-268][^cite_note-269][^cite_note-\:27-265] On 4 March, he converted from the penalty spot to put PSG 2–1 up against Lyon in the [2019–20 Coupe de France](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2019%E2%80%9320_Coupe_de_France) semi-finals; PSG went on to win 5–1.[^cite_note-270] PSG won the domestic treble, with Neymar scoring the club's lone goal in the [Coupe de France final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020_Coupe_de_France_Final) on 24 July to win 1–0.[^cite_note-CDF_2020-271][^cite_note-BBC_Sport-272][^cite_note-273] Despite official Ligue 1 awards not being given by the [UNFP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Troph%C3%A9es_UNFP_du_football) due to the impact of the [COVID-19 pandemic](62750956.md), Neymar was honoured by *L'Équipe* as the Best Player in Ligue 1 for 2019–20.[^cite_note-\:26-274] He was also included in both *L'Équipe*'s and *France Football*'s Ligue 1 Team of the Season.[^cite_note-275][^cite_note-276]

During the knockout stages of the Champions League, Neymar was hailed as "a leader", dictating play and contributing with two goals and two assists against [Borussia Dortmund](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Borussia_Dortmund), [Atalanta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Atalanta_BC) and [RB Leipzig](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RB_Leipzig) en route to PSG reaching its first ever [Champions League final](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020_UEFA_Champions_League_Final) to face Bayern Munich on 23 August.[^cite_note-277][^cite_note-278][^cite_note-279] He was praised as the best player in the world, including by former long-time [Arsenal](2174.md) manager [Arsène Wenger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ars%C3%A8ne_Wenger).[^cite_note-\:18-280][^cite_note-\:17-281][^cite_note-iNews2020-282] In the final, PSG lost 1–0.[^cite_note-\:38-283][^cite_note-espn_23_August_2020-284] With his ambition of guiding PSG to its first ever Champions League title, Neymar was visibly distraught at full time.[^cite_note-285]

Despite another injury-hit season with PSG, 2019–20 was one of Neymar's most successful seasons of his career.[^cite_note-286][^cite_note-287] He was named in the [UEFA Team of the Year](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA_Team_of_the_Year) for the first time since the 2014–15 season and was also included in *L'Équipe*'s world team of the year for the third time in his career.[^cite_note-\:68-288][^cite_note-UEFAErrors-101] *[The Independent](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Independent)* included him in their Champions League team of the tournament.[^cite_note-289] Neymar finished second behind [Robert Lewandowski](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_Lewandowski) in the fan vote for the [2020 The Best FIFA Men's Player](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Best_FIFA_Football_Awards_2020#The_Best_FIFA_Men's_Player) award, but a lack of votes from coaches, national team captains and journalists resulted in a finish outside the top five.[^cite_note-290]

#### 2020–21: Goalscoring records, Champions League heroics and Ligue 1 title race

Early in [2020–21](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020%E2%80%9321_Paris_Saint-Germain_FC_season), Neymar entered [PSG's top ten goalscorers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Paris_Saint-Germain_F.C._records_and_statistics#All-time_top_scorers) as he scored his 72nd goal for the club in a 6–1 win against Angers on October 3, in which he scored twice and assisted a goal.[^cite_note-\:27-265] In November, he reached 50 goals in Ligue 1 in 58 matches, making him the fastest player in the 21st century and the third-fastest player ever to do so, surpassing the likes of forwards [Zlatan Ibrahimović](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zlatan_Ibrahimovi%C4%87) and [Radamel Falcao](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Radamel_Falcao).[^cite_note-291] Like the previous season, he was largely influential for PSG in the [Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020%E2%80%9321_UEFA_Champions_League), where they lost two of their opening three games in the group stage, being at risk of being eliminated.[^cite_note-TNTGeo-292] In PSG's final three games in their group, Neymar converted a penalty as his side won 1–0 over Leipzig, scored twice in their 3–1 victory against Manchester United at [Old Trafford](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Old_Trafford), and netted a hat-trick in their 5–1 win against [İstanbul Başakşehir](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/%C4%B0stanbul_Ba%C5%9Fak%C5%9Fehir_F.K.).[^cite_note-293][^cite_note-294][^cite_note-295] His goals made him the first player in [European Cup](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Cup) and Champions League history to reach 20 goals for two different clubs, having previously done so with Barcelona.[^cite_note-\:51-296] He also became the fourth-quickest player to reach 40 goals in the Champions League (65 matches).[^cite_note-UEFAErrors-101] His third goal against Başakşehir was named by UEFA as the best goal of the 2020–21 Champions League group stage.[^cite_note-\:272-297]





The duo of Neymar and [Kylian Mbappé](48711857.md) were influential to PSG's run to the [2020–21 Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020%E2%80%9321_UEFA_Champions_League) semi-finals.

On 13 January 2021, Neymar scored from a penalty in PSG's 2–1 win over rivals Marseille to win the [Trophée des Champions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Troph%C3%A9e_des_Champions).[^cite_note-298][^cite_note-299] He soon suffered his third injury of the season, causing him to miss the round of 16 tie against Barcelona in the Champions League.[^cite_note-300][^cite_note-301][^cite_note-302] Neymar was available for the quarter-final victory against defending champions Bayern Munich in April, registering two assists to help PSG progress to the semi-finals to face Man City in May.[^cite_note-303][^cite_note-304] Reflecting on Neymar, Balagué opinionated that his performance in the first 45 minutes of the second leg against Bayern Munich was "the best individual performance of the season".[^cite_note-305] Ahead of the semi-final, the duo of Neymar and Mbappé was viewed as the main threat to Man City.[^cite_note-306][^cite_note-TNTGeo-292] PSG's wait for a first Champions League title continued as they were knocked out by Man City.[^cite_note-307]

In May, towards the end of the season, Neymar was crucial in Ligue 1 amid the title race with Lille, scoring a goal and assisting another in a 2–1 victory over [Lens](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RC_Lens) to return PSG back to the top of the league table.[^cite_note-308] He went on to open the scoring against Rennes with a penalty in an eventual 1–1 draw and opened the scoring with a penalty again in a 4–0 win over [Reims](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stade_de_Reims).[^cite_note-309][^cite_note-310] On 23 May, however, Lille pipped PSG to the title by just one point, preventing them from winning a record-tying 10th league title.[^cite_note-311] Despite this, they won the [Coupe de France](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2020%E2%80%9321_Coupe_de_France), which was Neymar's third and final cup with PSG.[^cite_note-312]

Neymar was nominated for the [2021 Ballon d'Or](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021_Ballon_d'Or), which was his ninth nomination — a joint record for a Brazilian player.[^cite_note-\:27-265][^cite_note-\:27-265] At the end of the season, the CIES Football Observatory, with support of performance data by [InStat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/InStat), concluded that Neymar ranked second in their list of the top 10 players in Europe during 2020–21 ahead of Lewandowski and only behind Messi, simultaneously being recognised as the best player in Ligue 1.[^cite_note-\:58-313][^cite_note-\:64-314] Earlier on in the year, the [IFFHS](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IFFHS) ranked him only behind Messi, Ronaldo, and [Andrés Iniesta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andr%C3%A9s_Iniesta) for their [World's Best Player of the Decade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/IFFHS_World's_Best_Player#The_Best_Man_Player_of_the_Decade_\(2011%E2%80%932020\)) award.[^cite_note-\:44-315]

On 8 May, Neymar extended his contract with PSG until 2025.[^cite_note-316] Upon this extension, he said he wanted to achieve PSG's biggest dream, to win the Champions League.[^cite_note-\:24-317]

#### 2021–23: 100th goal for PSG, return to top form and departure



Neymar (right) alongside Messi and [Kylian Mbappé](48711857.md) in a league fixture against [Marseille](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olympique_de_Marseille) (*[Le Classique](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Classique)*) in October 2021

Ahead of the [2021–22 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021%E2%80%9322_Paris_Saint-Germain_F.C._season), PSG had acquired several new players, including Neymar's former teammate Messi and longtime Real Madrid defender [Sergio Ramos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sergio_Ramos), leading the club to be considered genuine favourites to win the [2021–22 Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2021%E2%80%9322_UEFA_Champions_League).[^cite_note-318][^cite_note-TNTGeo-292] The arrival of Messi also saw the formation of a new attacking trio of Mbappé, Neymar, and Messi, dubbed by the media as *MNM*.[^cite_note-319]

Neymar scored his first goal of the season on 19 September, converting a penalty in a 2–1 win over Lyon.[^cite_note-320] He scored his 400th career goal on 6 November against Bordeaux, where he scored a brace and helped the team win the match 3–2.[^cite_note-321] On 28 November, Neymar suffered an ankle injury in a 3–1 win against Saint-Étienne.[^cite_note-\:75-322] The next day, PSG announced that Neymar would be unavailable for up to eight weeks.[^cite_note-\:75-322]

On 15 February 2022, Neymar returned in the round of 16 first leg in the Champions League as PSG faced Real Madrid.[^cite_note-\:76-323] He came off the bench in the second half and assisted Mbappé with a backheel pass to help his team win 1–0.[^cite_note-\:76-323] On 3 April, Neymar scored twice in a 5–1 victory against [Lorient](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FC_Lorient) before scoring a hat-trick six days later on 9 April, as PSG defeated [Clermont](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Clermont_Foot_63) 6–1.[^cite_note-324][^cite_note-325] On 21 May, he scored his 100th goal for PSG in a 5–0 win over [Metz](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FC_Metz), making him one of the few players to have scored at least 100 goals for three different clubs.[^cite_note-326][^cite_note-327] Neymar helped PSG win their record-tying 10th Ligue 1 title, though with 13 goals and 8 assists across all competitions, this was his lowest output since arriving in Europe in 2013.[^cite_note-328][^cite_note-329]

&#x20;Neymar drew criticism for his decline in performance compared to his previous seasons.[^cite_note-330][^cite_note-TNTGeo-292] He notably scored just three goals in Ligue 1 by the time of the lengthy injury sustained in November and scored no goals in the entire Champions League campaign for the first time in his career.[^cite_note-331] In the previous year, he suggested that he was growing tired of the intense demands of European football.[^cite_note-\:2-332] He was linked with a potential departure from PSG, but ultimately remained at the club.[^cite_note-Newseason-333] Ahead of the new season, he said his ambitions included winning the Champions League, further saying, “It must be with Paris. I have a contract with PSG, so there is no other choice.”[^cite_note-334]



Neymar celebrating with Mbappé after scoring in Ligue 1 in August 2022

Neymar started the [2022–23 season](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022%E2%80%9323_Paris_Saint-Germain_FC_season) in tremendous form, scoring twice against Nantes on 31 July in PSG's 4–0 result to win the Trophée des Champions, scoring a goal and providing a hat-trick of assists in a 5–0 win against Clermont on 6 August, scoring twice in a 5–2 win against Montpellier, and scoring twice and registering three more assists in a 7–1 away win over Lille.[^cite_note-335][^cite_note-336][^cite_note-337][^cite_note-338] He was named Ligue 1's [Player of the Month](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UNFP_Player_of_the_Month) in August.[^cite_note-Newseason-333][^cite_note-unfp.org-339] On 10 September, he surpassed PSG's former striker [Pauleta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pauleta) to become the club's fourth-top scorer of all time, scoring his 110th goal in a 1–0 win in the league against [Brest](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Stade_Brestois_29).[^cite_note-340] On 16 October, he scored in a 1–0 win against Marseille, taking PSG three points clear at the top of the league table.[^cite_note-TNTGeo-292] Neymar helped PSG extend their lead over Lens at the top of the table, scoring the goal to put PSG 3–2 ahead in an eventual 4–3 victory against [Troyes](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ES_Troyes_AC), while also assisting a goal.[^cite_note-341] Neymar was influential in Ligue 1 again on 8 November, when he produced a goal and an assist to help PSG defeat Lorient, winning 2–1.[^cite_note-\:27-265]

In the group stage of the [Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022%E2%80%9323_UEFA_Champions_League), Neymar registered 2 goals and 2 assists against Juventus, [Maccabi Haifa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maccabi_Haifa_F.C.) and [Benfica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/S.L._Benfica), helping PSG advance to the knockout stage.[^cite_note-UEFAErrors-101][^cite_note-342] Neymar had returned to his best form and was considered the most in-form player in the world, scoring 15 goals and providing 11 assists in just 20 games.[^cite_note-\:11-343][^cite_note-\:28-344][^cite_note-345][^cite_note-\:65-346] Ahead of the [2022 World Cup](17742072.md) that would take place during the winter, both English football pundits [Jamie Redknapp](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jamie_Redknapp) and [Gary Neville](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gary_Neville) tipped him to be the star player of the tournament.[^cite_note-SkyWC2022-347] He was also an early favourite for the [2023 Ballon d'Or](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023_Ballon_d'Or).[^cite_note-\:65-346] In January 2023, Neymar was announced as one of the fourteen players nominated for the [2022 The Best FIFA Men's Player](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Best_FIFA_Football_Awards_2022#The_Best_FIFA_Men's_Player) award.[^cite_note-nominees-348]



Neymar during a [Coupe de France](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022%E2%80%9323_Coupe_de_France) match against [Pays de Cassel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/US_Pays_de_Cassel) in January 2023

On 19 February, Neymar suffered a season-ending ankle injury against Lille in a Ligue 1 fixture, in which he scored PSG's second goal in a dramatic 4–3 comeback victory.[^cite_note-349][^cite_note-350] Following PSG's elimination by Bayern Munich in the Champions League round of 16 on 8 March, Neymar and Messi faced heavy criticism from sections of the French media and PSG supporters; although he missed the second leg, his performance in the first leg — along with other PSG players — was criticised.[^cite_note-351][^cite_note-352] On 27 May, PSG won Ligue 1 by just one point ahead of Lens; this was Neymar's fifth and final league title in France, contributing with 13 goals and 11 assists in 20 matches.[^cite_note-353][^cite_note-354]

At the end of the season, PSG began a squad overhaul, with Neymar being one of several players not being in the team's plans for the following season.[^cite_note-355] Neymar was linked with a potential return to Barcelona, however, Barcelona's hierarchy never reached an advanced stage to discuss finer economic details and possibilities, as the club's manager Xavi was not keen on signing him, leading both clubs to fail to reach an agreement.[^cite_note-356]

Neymar has been credited as a legend of PSG,[^cite_note-357][^cite_note-358] trailing only Ibrahimović, Cavani, and Mbappé in the club's top scorers at the time of his departure, and one of Ligue 1's greatest players.[^cite_note-ligue1.com-359][^cite_note-360][^cite_note-\:33-230] However, his tenure at PSG was marred by injuries, hindering his chances of helping the club win its first Champions League title.[^cite_note-361][^cite_note-FourFourTwo2025-362] He missed 119 matches for PSG in total as a result of being injured around 20 times across his six-year spell, showing a fragility that was not seen either at Barcelona or Santos.[^cite_note-Le_Monde.fr2-363][^cite_note-\:4-364] Despite this, he had the second-most goal contributions for the club during his six seasons there (188), only behind Mbappé.[^cite_note-\:57-365]

Following PSG's first Champions League title win in [2024–25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024%E2%80%9325_UEFA_Champions_League), their captain [Marquinhos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Marquinhos) dedicated the triumph to previous players at the club, including Neymar.[^cite_note-366]

### Al Hilal

On 15 August 2023, Neymar completed a transfer to [Saudi Pro League](4525043.md) club [Al Hilal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Hilal_SFC) on a two-year contract, joining a list of European league players who signed for the club, including [Rúben Neves](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/R%C3%BAben_Neves), [Kalidou Koulibaly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kalidou_Koulibaly), and [Sergej Milinković-Savić](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sergej_Milinkovi%C4%87-Savi%C4%87).[^cite_note-367][^cite_note-368][^cite_note-AJ-Hilal-369] The transfer fee was reported to be worth €90 million, making him the most expensive purchase in Saudi Pro League history.[^cite_note-AJ-Hilal-369] He was also reportedly being paid €150m a year at Al Hilal, six times the amount he earned at PSG.[^cite_note-370] According to *The Independent*, Barcelona was the only European club that showed genuine interest in signing Neymar; other top clubs turned away primarily due to his high salary and the expected transfer fee to sign him.[^cite_note-371]

Some media outlets criticised Neymar for choosing to not remain in a top European league and dubbed him as the 'Prince who never became King' due to leaving Europe without being crowned the world's best player with the Ballon d'Or.[^cite_note-\:62-372][^cite_note-\:72-373] However, Neymar declared, "I wouldn't be surprised if the Saudi league is better than the French", in light of fellow star players joining the league, including Ronaldo and [Karim Benzema](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karim_Benzema).[^cite_note-\:5-374] As a marquee signing, Neymar's transfer to Al Hilal drew comparisons to former Brazil international [Rivellino](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rivellino)'s transfer to the club in 1978.[^cite_note-\:5-374]

#### 2023–25: First matches before injury and termination of contract



Neymar celebrating his first and only goal for [Al Hilal](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Hilal_SFC) in October 2023

On 15 September 2023, Neymar made his debut for Al Hilal in the league during a 6–1 thrashing of [Al-Riyadh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Riyadh_SC), coming off the bench and providing an assist to a goal by fellow Brazilian [Malcom](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malcom_\(footballer\)).[^cite_note-375] That same year, on 3 October, Neymar scored his first goal for the club in a 3–0 away win against [Nassaji Mazandaran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/F.C._Nassaji_Mazandaran) in an [AFC Champions League group stage](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023%E2%80%9324_AFC_Champions_League_group_stage) match.[^cite_note-376] He played five games for his new club before [rupturing his anterior cruciate ligament](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anterior_cruciate_ligament_injury) while on international duty in October 2023 and was out for the rest of the season.[^cite_note-ACL_injury-377][^cite_note-378] Al Hilal won the [2023–24 Saudi Pro League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2023%E2%80%9324_Saudi_Pro_League) title; despite Neymar playing just three games in the league, he collected his eighth career league title.[^cite_note-\:56-379]

Neymar returned to action for [2024–25](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024%E2%80%9325_Al_Hilal_SFC_season) on 21 October 2024 during Al Hilal's [AFC Champions League](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2024%E2%80%9325_AFC_Champions_League_Elite) match against [Al Ain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Ain_FC).[^cite_note-380] However, he soon suffered a hamstring injury that sidelined him for the rest of the year.[^cite_note-381] According to Al Hilal coach [Jorge Jesus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jorge_Jesus), Neymar could "no longer perform at the level we are used to" due to the lengthy injuries.[^cite_note-382] On 27 January 2025, the club announced an agreement for the termination of Neymar's contract by mutual consent.[^cite_note-383] In a leaving message, Neymar said, "I gave it all to play, and I wish we could have enjoyed better moments together on the field".[^cite_note-384]

Neymar totalled one goal and two assists in seven appearances for Al Hilal;[^cite_note-385] considering his transfer fee and wages, various media outlets debated whether Neymar's transfer to the club is the worst transfer in football history.[^cite_note-386][^cite_note-387][^cite_note-388][^cite_note-389]

### Santos return

#### 2025: Injuries, match-winning performances and avoiding relegation

On 30 January 2025, Neymar announced through his social media that he would be signing a contract with his first club [Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santos_FC).[^cite_note-390][^cite_note-391] The following day, Santos confirmed his return.[^cite_note-392] One of the reasons for Neymar returning to Santos was to regain top form ahead of the [2026 World Cup](36581929.md).[^cite_note-393] He started wearing the number 10 jersey, the number which Pelé had worn during his time spent between 1956 and 1974.[^cite_note-394] Neymar made his re-debut for *Peixe* on 6 February, coming on as a half-time substitute for [Gabriel Bontempo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gabriel_Bontempo) in a 1–1 Campeonato Paulista home draw against [Botafogo-SP](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Botafogo_Futebol_Clube_\(SP\)).[^cite_note-395] According to [Reuters](18998750.md), "The Urbano Caldeira stadium erupted in celebration as jubilant and tearful fans lit up their mobile phones to give the striker, who left the club 12 years ago, a hero's welcome before kick-off."[^cite_note-396] He received the [Man of the Match](906698.md) award after the match.[^cite_note-397]



Neymar playing for [Santos](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Santos_FC) in November 2025

Neymar scored his first goal after returning ten days later, netting the opener in a 3–1 home win over [Água Santa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Esporte_Clube_%C3%81gua_Santa) through a penalty; he also assisted [Guilherme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Guilherme_\(footballer,_born_1995\)) in the club's third goal, and received another Man of the Match award.[^cite_note-398] On 23 February, Neymar scored directly from a [corner kick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Olimpico_goal) in a 3–0 win over [Inter de Limeira](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Associa%C3%A7%C3%A3o_Atl%C3%A9tica_Internacional_\(Limeira\)), and earned the Man of the Match award again.[^cite_note-399][^cite_note-400] With his physical fitness experiencing an improvement compared to his time at Al-Hilal,[^cite_note-401] he would make his Série A debut on 13 March, coming on as a substitute 1–0 loss against [Fluminense](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fluminense_FC).
